D. x=-2, y=-3

请将答案放在 \boxed{} 中。
Response

\[
2\vec a+y\vec b=x\vec a-3\vec b
\]

\[
(2-x)\vec a+(y+3)\vec b=0
\]

因为 \(\vec a,\vec b\) 不共线，所以系数必须都为 0：
\[
2-x=0,\quad y+3=0
\]
因此：
\[
x=2,\quad y=-3
\]

最终答案：
\[
\boxed{\text{A. }x=2,\ y=-3}
\]

gaokao_000_005 ✓ B B ✓ ✓ 9601 https://api.deepseek.com

请解答以下数学题。逐步推理，将最终答案放在 \boxed{} 中。

6. 已知函数f(x)=\frac{x+2}{e^x+a}的最大值为1，则a = （ ）
A. \frac{1}{2}
B. 1
C. \frac{3}{2}
D. 2

请将答案放在 \boxed{} 中。
Response
由选项可知 \(a>0\)，所以 \(e^x+a>0\)。

设  
\[
g(x)=x+2-e^x
\]
则  
\[
g'(x)=1-e^x
\]
所以 \(g(x)\) 在 \(x=0\) 处取最大值：
\[
g(0)=0+2-e^0=1
\]
因此 \(g(x)\le 1\) 恒成立。

因为 \(f(x)\) 的最大值为 \(1\)：

1. 对任意 \(x\)，
\[
\frac{x+2}{e^x+a}\le 1
\]
即
\[
x+2\le e^x+a
\]
\[
a\ge x+2-e^x=g(x)
\]
所以
\[
a\ge 1.
\]

2. 存在 \(x_0\) 使 \(f(x_0)=1\)，即
\[
\frac{x_0+2}{e^{x_0}+a}=1
\]
\[
a=x_0+2-e^{x_0}=g(x_0)\le 1.
\]

因此
\[
a=1.
\]

验证：当 \(a=1\) 时，\(f(0)=1\)，且 \(f(x)\le 1\)，满足题意。

\[
\boxed{B}
\]
Reasoning
We need answer in Chinese likely. Need solve. Need be careful: Problem: f(x)= (x+2)/(e^x + a) max value 1, find a. Options. Need determine. Need rigorous.

We need solve. Let's analyze.

Function f(x) = (x+2)/(e^x + a). Max value is 1. Need a? Domain? Denominator not zero. Usually if maximum is 1, then (x+2)/(e^x+a) <= 1 for all x in domain and equality attainable for some x. Need solve for a.

Need consider denominator sign can vary depending a. e^x >0.

Let's set y = x+2 maybe. Let t = x+2. e^x = e^{t-2}= e^{-2} e^t. f = t/(C e^t + a), C=e^{-2}. Max =1.

Alternative define g(x)= f(x)-1 = (x+2 - e^x - a)/(e^x+a). Max=1 at x0. At maximum x0, f(x0)=1 -> x0+2 = e^{x0}+a -> a = x0+2 - e^{x0}. Need also f(x)<=1 for all domain and inequality signs depend denominator.

Need determine possible a and x0. Also f maximum value 1 means global maximum exists.

Let's explore. Let h(x)=x+2-e^x. Max of h? h'=1-e^x. Max at x=0: h=2-1=1, value 1. e^x+a maybe.

Observation: The numerator x+2 and e^x. For equality f=1: x+2 = e^x + a, so a = h(x) = x+2-e^x. Since h max is 1, maximum possible a at x=0 is 1. But a? Could be less.

Need sign constraints. If denominator negative, inequality direction reverses. Also must not be zero.

Let's examine.

f(x) = n/d where n=x+2, d=e^x+a.

We need max =1.

First, f(x0)=1 implies d(x0)=n(x0) >0? Since d = n at x0. n=x0+2 could be positive? If n negative, d negative equal, possible f=1 but x0<-2. But d=e^x+a maybe negative; maybe not max? Let's find.

At f=1, n=d. h=a. h ranges? h(x)=x+2-e^x. As x→-∞, h→ -∞? x+2 - e^x ~ x+2 -> -∞. Max at x=0=1; as x→∞, h→ -∞. Range (-∞,1]. Thus equality possible only a ≤1. If a=1, equality at x=0 unique? h max unique, yes n=d=2. If a<1, two roots? h concave? h'=1-e^x, h''=-e^x<0; concave, max 1, tends -∞, so for a<1, two roots, one <0, one >0. Need maximum 1 maybe at one root dependent denominator sign.

But if f has max 1, requires f(x)<=1 for all domain. Equivalent depending on sign of denominator: For d>0: n <= d -> h(x) >= a. Wait condition f ≤1 with d>0: n ≤ d -> x+2 ≤ e^x+a -> h(x) ≥ a, where h(x)=x+2-e^x. For d<0: inequality reverse: n ≥ d -> h(x) ≤ a. So:
- On intervals where d>0, need h(x) ≥ a for all x in those intervals.
- On intervals where d<0, need h(x) ≤ a for all x in those intervals.
At equality points h=a.

Since h≤1. For a maybe. Need evaluate.

设 \(p(x)=x+2-e^x\). 由选项 \(a>0\)，故 \(e^x+a>0\)，不等式方向不变。

因最大值为 1：

\[

\]

求 p max:
\[
p'(x)=1-e^x,\quad p''(x)=-e^x<0
\]
最大值在 x=0:
\[
p(0)=0+2-1=1.
\]
因此 \(a\ge1\).

2. 最大值能达到，所以存在 \(x_0\) 使 \(f(x_0)=1\):
\[

\]
故 \(a\le1\).

所以 \(a=1\). 验证：当 \(a=1\)，\(f(x)\le1\) 等价于 \(p(x)\le1\)，成立；且 \(f(0)=1\)，故最大值确为 1.

\[
x,\ x+d,\ x+2d,\ x+3d,\ x+4d,\ x+5d
\]

则

\[
6x+15d=108
\]

检验选项：

\[
8,12,16,20,24,28

\[
(3,5),\ (1,11),\ (3,13),\ (5,15),\ (7,17),\ (9,19)
\]

对应和正好为 \(8,12,16,20,24,28\)。

所以 \(d=4\)，选 B。

\[
\boxed{B}

请解答以下数学题。逐步推理，将最终答案放在 \boxed{} 中。

8. 设U={(x_1, x_2, x_3)|x_i \in \{-2,-1,1,2\}, i=1,2,3}\}为空间中64个点构成的集合，点P(1,1,1)，记样本空间\Omega = C_U^P，从\Omega中随机取一个点，定义随机变量X如下：对\Omega中的每个点A(x_1, x_2, x_3)，令X(A) = x_1 + x_2 + x_3，则X的数学期望值为（ ）
A. -\frac{1}{21}
B. -\frac{1}{63}
C. 0
D. \frac{1}{7}

请将答案放在 \boxed{} 中。
Response
由题意，\(\Omega=C_U^P\) 表示从 \(U\) 中去掉点 \(P\)，即  
\[
\Omega=U\setminus\{P\},\qquad |\Omega|=64-1=63.
\]

在 \(U\) 中，每一个坐标取到 \(-2,-1,1,2\) 的次数均为 \(4^2=16\)，所以每个坐标在 \(U\) 上的总和为  
\[
16(-2-1+1+2)=0.
\]
因此所有点坐标和的总和为 \(0\)。

点 \(P(1,1,1)\) 的坐标和为 \(3\)，所以 \(\Omega\) 中所有点坐标和的总和为  
\[
0-3=-3.
\]

故期望值为  
\[
E(X)=\frac{-3}{63}=-\frac{1}{21}.
\]

\[
\boxed{-\frac{1}{21}}
\]

答案选 **A**。

Format correct
True
Prompt
请解答以下数学题。逐步推理，将最终答案放在 \boxed{} 中。

10. 在空间中，A、B为两个定点，动点C到直线AB的距离为2，动点D到直线AB的距离为1。若二面角C - AB - D为60°，则（ ）
A. \angle CAD \ge 60^\circ
B. CD \ge \sqrt{3}
C. 当AB \perp CD时，CD \perp平面ABD
D. 当AB \perp平面ACD时，AC \perp AD

请将答案放在 \boxed{} 中。
Response
设 \(AB\) 的方向单位向量为 \(\mathbf e\)，取 \(A\) 为原点。令  
\[
\overrightarrow{AC}=x\mathbf e+\mathbf c,\qquad 
\overrightarrow{AD}=y\mathbf e+\mathbf d
\]
其中 \(\mathbf c,\mathbf d\perp \mathbf e\)，且 \(|\mathbf c|=2,|\mathbf d|=1\)。  
因为二面角为 \(60^\circ\)，所以
\[
\mathbf c\cdot \mathbf d=2\cdot 1\cdot \cos60^\circ=1.
\]

**A 不成立**：取  
\[
A=(0,0,0),\ B=(3,0,0),\ C=(1,2,0),\ D=\left(2,\frac12,\frac{\sqrt3}{2}\right),
\]
满足条件。但
\[
\cos\angle CAD=\frac{(1,2,0)\cdot(2,\frac12,\frac{\sqrt3}{2})}{\sqrt5\cdot\sqrt5}
=\frac35>\frac12,
\]
所以 \(\angle CAD<60^\circ\)。

**B 成立**：
\[
CD^2=(y-x)^2+|\mathbf d-\mathbf c|^2
=(y-x)^2+1+4-2\mathbf c\cdot\mathbf d
=(y-x)^2+3\ge3,
\]
故 \(CD\ge \sqrt3\)。

**C 成立**：若 \(AB\perp CD\)，则 \(y=x\)。设 \(y=x=t\)，则
\[
\overrightarrow{CD}\cdot \mathbf e=0,\qquad
\overrightarrow{CD}\cdot \overrightarrow{AD}
=(\mathbf d-\mathbf c)\cdot(t\mathbf e+\mathbf d)
=|\mathbf d|^2-\mathbf c\cdot\mathbf d=1-1=0.
\]
所以 \(CD\) 垂直于平面 \(ABD\)。

**D 不成立**：若 \(AB\perp\) 平面 \(ACD\)，则 \(x=y=0\)，于是 \(\angle CAD=60^\circ\)，不是 \(90^\circ\)。

因此正确选项为：

\[
\boxed{BC}
\]

5. C true:
Assume \(AB\perp CD\). Since AB direction e=(1,0,0), this means \(x=y\) (write same t). Then
\[
\overrightarrow{CD}=(0,\mathbf v-\mathbf u),\quad 
\overrightarrow{AD}=(t,\mathbf v).
\]
\[
\overrightarrow{CD}\cdot \overrightarrow{AD}
=(\mathbf v-\mathbf u)\cdot \mathbf v
=|\mathbf v|^2-\mathbf u\cdot\mathbf v=1-1=0.
\]
Also \(\overrightarrow{CD}\cdot (1,0,0)=0\). Therefore CD is perpendicular to both AD and a line through D parallel to AB, which are two intersecting directions in plane ABD. Hence CD⊥平面ABD. Need line "through D parallel to AB" direction e; but the vector (1,0,0) is not necessarily through D, but direction. In vector terms, CD perpendicular to two nonparallel direction vectors spanning plane ABD: e_x and \overrightarrow{AD}. If a line is perpendicular to two intersecting lines in a plane, it is perpendicular to plane. Since CD intersects plane at D (CD contains D), okay. We can state "CD 的方向与平面 ABD 内两个不平行方向 e_x, AD 都垂直，故 CD⊥平面ABD." Good.

Let's write final elegantly:

```
设
\[
\overrightarrow{AC}=x e+\mathbf c,\quad
\overrightarrow{AD}=y e+\mathbf d
\]
其中 \(\mathbf c,\mathbf d\perp e\), \(|\mathbf c|=2, |\mathbf d|=1\)。由二面角为 \(60^\circ\)，有 \(\mathbf c\cdot\mathbf d=1\)。
```

Then:

**A**:
```
取 \(A=(0,0,0),B=(3,0,0),C=(1,2,0),D=(2,\frac12,\frac{\sqrt3}{2})\)。其满足条件。而 \(|AC|=|AD|=\sqrt5\)，\(\overrightarrow{AC}\cdot\overrightarrow{AD}=3\)，所以 \(\cos\angle CAD=3/5>1/2\)，故 \(\angle CAD<60^\circ\)。A 不成立。
```
Check \(|AC|\) and \(|AD|\) both √5, yes. \(AC·AD=3\). Great.

**B**:
```
\[
CD^2=|(y-x)e+(\mathbf d-\mathbf c)|^2
=(y-x)^2+|\mathbf d-\mathbf c|^2
=(y-x)^2+|\mathbf d|^2+|\mathbf c|^2-2\mathbf c\cdot\mathbf d
=(y-x)^2+1+4-2\ge3.
\]
```
Therefore true.

**C**:
```
若 \(AB\perp CD\)，则 \((y-x)e+(\mathbf d-\mathbf c)\) 与 \(e\) 点乘为0，所以 \(y=x\)。设 \(y=x=t\)。此时平面 \(ABD\) 由 \(e\) 与 \(\overrightarrow{AD}=t e+\mathbf d\) 张成，且
\[
\overrightarrow{CD}\cdot e=0,\quad
\overrightarrow{CD}\cdot \overrightarrow{AD}
=(\mathbf d-\mathbf c)\cdot(t e+\mathbf d)
=|\mathbf d|^2-\mathbf c\cdot\mathbf d=0.
\]
故 \(CD\) 垂直于平面 \(ABD\)。C 成立。
```
Maybe "点乘为0" if \(AB⊥CD\), \(\overrightarrow{CD}\) dot with e =0; yes.
Need "e and AD nonparallel" maybe:
```
且 \(e\) 与 \(\overrightarrow{AD}\) 不平行，所以可作为平面内两相交方向。
```
Add.

**D**:
```
若 \(AB\perp平面ACD\)，则 \(e\cdot\overrightarrow{AC}=x=0,\ e\cdot\overrightarrow{AD}=y=0\)，所以 \(\angle CAD=\angle(\mathbf c,\mathbf d)=60^\circ\)，不是 \(90^\circ\)，D 不成立。
```
Maybe "∠CAD = ∠(c,d)" because \(AC=\mathbf c, AD=\mathbf d\). Good.

Prompt
请解答以下数学题。逐步推理，将最终答案放在 \boxed{} 中。

11. 已知圆C_1:(x+1)^2+y^2=1，圆C_2:(x-1)^2+y^2=1，圆C_3:x^2+(y-\sqrt{3})^2=1，直线l:y=kx+b与C_1, C_2, C_3均有两个交点。记l被C_1, C_2, C_3截得的弦长分别为s_1、s_2、s_3，则（ ）
A. k可以取任意实数
B. 满足s_1=s_2=s_3的直线l共有3条
C. 满足s_1+s_2+s_3=3的直线l多于3条
D. 当b=0时，s_1+s_2+s_3的最大值为\frac{2\sqrt{21}}{3}

请将答案放在 \boxed{} 中。
Response
设圆心分别为  
\(A(-1,0),B(1,0),C(0,\sqrt3)\)，圆心到直线 \(l\) 的距离为 \(d_i\)，则  
\[
s_i=2\sqrt{1-d_i^2}.
\]

**A 错：**  
取 \(k=\frac1{\sqrt3}\)，此时 \(\sqrt{1+k^2}=\frac2{\sqrt3}\)。若三个圆都与直线有两个交点，需同时有  
\[
b\in\left(-\frac1{\sqrt3},\sqrt3\right),\quad
b\in\left(-\sqrt3,\frac1{\sqrt3}\right),\quad
b\in\left(\frac1{\sqrt3},\frac5{\sqrt3}\right).
\]
前两个区间交集为 \(\left(-\frac1{\sqrt3},\frac1{\sqrt3}\right)\)，与第三个区间无公共部分，因此不是任意 \(k\) 都可取。

**B 对：**  
若 \(s_1=s_2=s_3\)，则 \(d_1=d_2=d_3\)。带符号距离中至少有两个相等。对应三条中位线：
\[
y=\frac{\sqrt3}{2},\qquad y=\sqrt3x,\qquad y=-\sqrt3x.
\]
它们的圆心距都是 \(\frac{\sqrt3}{2}<1\)，弦长都为 \(1\)，且只有这 3 条。

**C 对：**  
上述 3 条线都有 \(s_1+s_2+s_3=3\)。另外取  
\[
y=\pm \frac{\sqrt{171}}5x
\]
时，有
\[
s_1=s_2=\frac57,\qquad s_3=\frac{11}{7},
\]
所以
\[
s_1+s_2+s_3=3.
\]
因此满足条件的直线多于 3 条。

**D 对：**  
当 \(b=0\) 时，令 \(r=\sqrt{1+k^2}\)，需 \(r^2>3\)。则
\[
s_1=s_2=\frac2r,\qquad s_3=\frac{2\sqrt{r^2-3}}r.
\]
令 \(v=\sqrt{r^2-3}\ge0\)，则
\[
S=s_1+s_2+s_3=\frac{4+2v}{\sqrt{v^2+3}}.
\]
求导得
\[
S'(v)=\frac{6-4v}{(v^2+3)^{3/2}},
\]
故 \(v=\frac32\) 时最大，最大值为
\[
\frac{4+3}{\sqrt{\frac94+3}}
=\frac{7}{\sqrt{21}/2}
=\frac{2\sqrt{21}}3.
\]

所以正确的是：

\[
\boxed{BCD}
\]

Let's set up coordinates more cleanly. Use signed distance h_i from centers to line (normal unit N, line N·X=p); h_i=N·P_i - p. Chords s_i=2√(1-h_i^2) if |h_i|<1. Condition all |h_i|<1 imposes p interval and N orientation.

The h_i are affine functions of p with fixed differences independent of p:
h_A = N·A - p = -N_x - p.
h_B = N·B - p = N_x - p.
h_C = N·C - p = √3 N_y - p.

Let define N=(cos θ, sin θ). Differences:
h_A - h_B = -2 N_x.
h_A - h_C = -N_x - √3 N_y.
h_B - h_C = N_x - √3 N_y.
For given θ, as p varies, all h_i shift by -p; absolute values.

Maybe express in barycentric coordinates of p relative to projections of centers on normal. Let projections q_A=N·A, q_B=N·B, q_C=N·C. h_i=q_i-p. So along the normal axis, the three centers project to q_i. A line with normal N at coordinate p has distances |q_i-p|. Prompt
请解答以下数学题。逐步推理，将最终答案放在 \boxed{} 中。

12. 双曲线5x^2 - 6y^2 = 1的离心率为__________.

请将答案放在 \boxed{} 中。
Response
将方程化为标准式：

\[
5x^2-6y^2=1 \Rightarrow \frac{x^2}{\frac15}-\frac{y^2}{\frac16}=1
\]

所以

\[
a^2=\frac15,\quad b^2=\frac16
\]

对双曲线：

\[
c^2=a^2+b^2=\frac15+\frac16=\frac{11}{30}
\]

离心率：

\[
e=\frac ca=\sqrt{\frac{c^2}{a^2}}
=\sqrt{\frac{\frac{11}{30}}{\frac15}}
=\sqrt{\frac{11}{6}}
=\frac{\sqrt{66}}{6}
\]

\[
\boxed{\frac{\sqrt{66}}{6}}
\]
Reasoning

Here 5x^2 -6y^2=1 => x^2/(1/5) - y^2/(1/6)=1. So a^2=1/5, b^2=1/6. For hyperbola, c^2=a^2+b^2=1/5+1/6=11/30. Eccentricity e=c/a = sqrt(c^2/a^2)=sqrt((11/30)/(1/5))=sqrt(11/30*5)=sqrt(11/6). Since 11/6. Or e = sqrt(1+b^2/a^2) = sqrt(1+ (1/6)/(1/5)) = sqrt(1+5/6)=sqrt(11/6). Answer \boxed{\sqrt{11/6}}. Need perhaps mention.

Prompt
请解答以下数学题。逐步推理，将最终答案放在 \boxed{} 中。

13. 已知f(x)=2sin(ax+\theta) (a \in Z, 0 \le \theta < 2\pi)是偶函数，f(x)在区间(0, \frac{\pi}{2})单调递增。则\theta =__________，f(\frac{2\pi}{3}) =__________.

请将答案放在 \boxed{} 中。
Response
由偶函数 \(f(-x)=f(x)\)，得
\[
\sin(\theta-ax)=\sin(\theta+ax)
\]
展开得
\[
-2\cos\theta\sin(ax)=0.
\]
若 \(a=0\)，则 \(f(x)\) 为常数，不可能单调递增，所以 \(a\ne 0\)。因此
\[
\cos\theta=0.
\]
又 \(0\le\theta<2\pi\)，所以
\[
\theta=\frac{\pi}{2}\quad\text{或}\quad\frac{3\pi}{2}.
\]

若 \(\theta=\frac{\pi}{2}\)，则
\[
f(x)=2\cos(ax),
\]
在 \(x=0\) 右侧函数下降，不合题意。故
\[
\theta=\frac{3\pi}{2}.
\]

此时
\[
f(x)=-2\cos(ax)=-2\cos(|a|x).
\]
由 \(f(x)\) 在 \((0,\frac{\pi}{2})\) 单调递增，可得 \(|a|=1\) 或 \(2\)。于是
\[
f\left(\frac{2\pi}{3}\right)
=-2\cos\left(\frac{2a\pi}{3}\right)
=1.
\]

\[
\boxed{\theta=\frac{3\pi}{2},\quad f\left(\frac{2\pi}{3}\right)=1}
\]

Prompt
请解答以下数学题。逐步推理，将最终答案放在 \boxed{} 中。

14. 设实数q满足：存在数列{a_n}，使得对于任意n \in N^*，均有a_1 + a_2 + \cdots + a_{3n} = n^2 + n，且{a_n}中有某连续9项a_k, a_{k+1}, \cdots, a_{k+8}是公比为q的等比数列。则q的最大值为__________.

请将答案放在 \boxed{} 中。
Response
设
\[
T_m=a_{3m-2}+a_{3m-1}+a_{3m}.
\]
由
\[
S_{3m}=m^2+m
\]
得
\[
T_1=2,\qquad T_m=S_{3m}-S_{3m-3}=2m\quad(m\ge2).
\]
所以
\[
T_m=2m.
\]

设连续9项为
\[
x,xq,\dots,xq^8.
\]
其中连续的3项之和为
\[
xq^t(1+q+q^2),
\]
且 \(q\in\mathbb R\) 时 \(1+q+q^2>0\)。

若 \(k=3r-2\)，则这9项正好包含三组完整的 \(T_r,T_{r+1},T_{r+2}\)。它们的和
\[
2r,\ 2r+2,\ 2r+4
\]
应成公比为 \(q^3\) 的等比数列，但
\[
(2r+2)^2\ne 2r(2r+4),
\]
矛盾。所以 \(k\not\equiv1\pmod3\)。

若 \(k=3r-1\) 或 \(k=3r\)，则这9项中包含完整两组 \(T_{r+1},T_{r+2}\)。由于它们在等比数列中相隔3项，和之比为 \(q^3\)，所以
\[
q^3=\frac{T_{r+2}}{T_{r+1}}
=\frac{2(r+2)}{2(r+1)}
=\frac{r+2}{r+1}\le \frac32.
\]
因此
\[
q\le \sqrt[3]{\frac32}.
\]

取 \(q=\sqrt[3]{\frac32}\) 时可构造数列达到等号，因此最大值为
\[
\boxed{\sqrt[3]{\frac32}}.
\]

We need solve problem in Chinese. Need answer in boxed. Need reason. Let's restate.

Often they define "如果一个数列从第2项起，每一项与它的前一项的比等于同一个常数，这个数列叫等比数列，这个常数叫公比，公比通常用字母q表示(q≠0)" because division by previous term and if one term zero then next? Let's formulate a rigorous proof perhaps in final. The final answer should be in Chinese. Need maybe "设三连项组和 T_m = a_{3m-2}+a_{3m-1}+a_{3m}" maybe T_m=2m. Let's denote T_m.

Detailed upper bound:

From condition:
For n=1, S_3=2.
For n≥2, T_n = S_{3n}-S_{3n-3} = (n^2+n)-((n-1)^2+(n-1)) = 2n.
Thus T_n=2n.

Assume a_k,...,a_{k+8} is GP with ratio q, write these as x, xq,...,xq^8. Since T_m positive and a full block of three GP terms has sum x q^s(1+q+q^2). For real q, 1+q+q^2 = (q+1/2)^2+3/4 >0, so x q^s ≠0 for any full block considered.

Now examine k modulo 3:
1. k≡1 mod3. Then positions k,...,k+8 are exactly three whole original blocks: suppose they are T_m,T_{m+1},T_{m+2}. The sums are
2m = x(1+q+q^2),
2m+2 = x q^3(1+q+q^2),
